# Database investigation rules

Coordinator database: `crypto_ai` on localhost:3307 as `ai_investigator`.
Do not mix with `crypto_ai_v2`.

## Credentials
Agents never receive the database password.
The coordinator/wrapper reads `AGENT_DB_PASSWORD` from the local environment only.
Do not write that value into AGENTS.md, COORDINATOR.md, Cursor rules, docs, prompts, logs, or source.

Other settings (defaults in parentheses):

- `AGENT_DB_HOST` (localhost)
- `AGENT_DB_PORT` (3307)
- `AGENT_DB_NAME` (crypto_ai)
- `AGENT_DB_USER` (ai_investigator)
- `AGENT_MYSQL_EXE` (local mysql.exe)

## Approved access path
Agents request evidence. The coordinator executes:

`scripts/agent-db-query.ps1`

The MySQL account is database-level read-only. The wrapper is an additional safety layer.

Allowed statement families: `SELECT`, `SHOW`, `DESCRIBE`/`DESC`, `EXPLAIN`.
Everything else is rejected by default, including writes, DDL, grants, CALL, SET, LOAD, locks, and transaction control.
Multiple statements are allowed only if the wrapper proves each statement independently read-only.

## Audit
Each query is stored under `agent-docs/runs/<run-id>/db/` with SQL, result, and `DATABASE_QUERY_LOG.md`.
Never log the password.

## Time
Raw MySQL/Binance timestamps are UTC. Never describe a raw DB timestamp as KSA.
When converting for a report:

- UTC: `<raw time>`
- KSA: `<UTC + 3h>`

## Registry
`agent-docs/DATABASE_ENTITY_REGISTRY.md` is durable entity knowledge.
One agent's discovery is not fact. Append only through dual-agent agreement (`AGREED`/`AGREED`).
Proposed future schema is not current schema.

## Scope examples
Harmless reads may cover `trade_signal`, trades/positions, candles, decision/analysis/market-context tables, Replay/test identifiers, and related trader entities in `crypto_ai` only.

## FINDINGS ƒ?" Iteration 1

1. **Scope is frozen:** only `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137** will be analysed. No substitutions will be made.

2. **No trade-specific DB evidence is available in this Codex-isolated evidence folder yet.** Therefore, no claim can presently be made about any tradeƒ?Ts symbol, entry, exit, loss cause, execution correctness, or protection behavior.

3. **The supplied snapshot timestamps conflict.**  
   - Supplied UTC: `2026-10-25 10:08:57`  
   - UTC + 3h would be KSA: `2026-10-25 13:08:57`  
   - Supplied KSA instead says: `2026-10-23 11:36:38`  
   
   These cannot represent the same instant. The six IDs remain authoritative despite this conflict; the study must not use the snapshot clock as a precise selection cutoff.

4. The source establishes the evidence path, pending verification against the actual DB schema:
   - `paper_position` records entry/exit prices, SL/TP, realized P&L, entry/exit signal links, close reason, and UTC lifecycle timestamps.
   - `production_exit_audit` is designed to preserve the actual close trigger separately from the market-context signal.
   - `wallet_trade` carries execution quantities, prices, fees, cost basis, and realized P&L.
   - `market_price_event` is required to evaluate intrabar protection; candle OHLC must not be substituted for absent accepted price-event evidence.

## EVIDENCE

### PROJECT EVIDENCE

- [PaperPosition.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/PaperPosition.java:10) maps the authoritative `paper_position` entity and its signal links, exit fields, reasons, and timestamps.
- [ProductionExitAudit.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/audit/domain/ProductionExitAudit.java:12) defines immutable diagnostic evidence for the terminal trigger and related position/signal context.
- [ProductionExitAuditService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/audit/service/ProductionExitAuditService.java:23) records the close trigger separately from the source signal.
- [PaperTradingService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:483) computes paper-position terminal P&L from remaining quantity and preserves any prior partial-harvest P&L.
- [LivePositionProtectionService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:392) writes live protection closures back to `paper_position` and records their audit evidence.
- [WalletTrade.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/domain/WalletTrade.java:11) defines fee, cost-basis, execution-price-observation, and realized-P&L evidence.
- The study charter designates `crypto_ai.paper_position` as authoritative and fixes the six position IDs.

### AGENT INFERENCE

- `paper_position.realized_pnl` alone cannot establish net financial P&L, because paper-position calculation does not itself include wallet trade fees. Wallet execution records are required for reconciliation.

## FINDINGS ƒ?" Iteration 4: Code / Trade Route

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The frozen scope remains only `paper_position` IDs `182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137`. No trade-specific DB result exists yet in the permitted Codex evidence folder, so none of the current source routes can be attributed to an individual frozen trade.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** A direct 1m Binance kline follows this current route: candle plus `market_price_event(PENDING)` input transaction ƒ+' separate protection transaction ƒ+' observer ƒ+' closed-candle dispatch. The protection transaction is deliberately before analysis dispatch. [BinanceKlineService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:101), [KlineTransactionCoordinator.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:40)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** For an accepted direct-price observation, protection locks the open managed position, rejects stale/future/out-of-order observations, evaluates TP, absolute SL, profit-lock, then normal multi-timeframe exit logic. A successful callback writes `market_price_event.delivery_status=APPLIED` in the same effects transaction; the `PRICE_OUTCOME_COMMITTED` log occurs only after transaction commit. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:53), [MarketPriceEventService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:80)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** On the live protection STOP_LOSS route, the wallet-managed position and its `wallet_trade` SELL are updated first; only if that mechanical wallet exit returns true does the service close the matching open `paper_position` and write an immutable `production_exit_audit` record. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:173), [WalletAutoExecutionService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletAutoExecutionService.java:475)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The signal-driven close route has a materially different order: `PaperTradingService.completeClose` persists `paper_position` first, then attempts the wallet exit and catches a wallet exception. Thus a selected closed paper row does not, by itself, prove a matching wallet execution; reconciliation against `wallet_trade` is mandatory. [PaperTradingService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:487)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The durable linkage is not numerical identity between `paper_position.id` and `wallet_managed_position.id`. It is `paper_position.signal_id ƒ+' wallet_managed_position.entry_signal_id`; `ProductionExitAuditService` uses that relationship when it records `wallet_position_id`. [ProductionExitAuditService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/audit/service/ProductionExitAuditService.java:37)

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** The Iteration-3 ICPUSDT log candidate (`WalletManagedPosition` ID `182`, event `1837489`) is consistent with a committed live STOP_LOSS route, but remains unlinked to frozen `paper_position` ID `182`. It must not be treated as evidence for that frozen trade until the linkage query succeeds.

- **[EXTERNAL RESEARCH]** None.

- `production_exit_audit` separately records the actual terminal trigger, source signal context, linked managed-position ID, and close price/SL/TP. [ProductionExitAudit.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/audit/domain/ProductionExitAudit.java:17)

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I4-001  
Purpose: Prove each frozen paper positionƒ?Ts durable route, including entry/exit signals, managed-position linkage, recorded terminal trigger, wallet execution evidence, and accepted/non-canonical price events around close. All timestamps returned are UTC.  
SQL:

```sql
SELECT
    pp.id AS paper_position_id,
    pp.symbol,
    pp.status,
    pp.quantity,
    pp.entry_price,
    pp.stop_loss,
    pp.take_profit,
    pp.exit_price,
    pp.realized_pnl,
    pp.signal_id AS entry_signal_id,
    pp.exit_signal_id,
    pp.close_reason,
    pp.exit_reason,
    pp.opened_at,
    pp.closed_at,
    es.interval_code AS entry_interval,
    es.decision AS entry_decision,
    es.original_decision AS entry_original_decision,
    es.latest_price AS entry_signal_price,
    es.generated_at AS entry_signal_generated_at,
    xs.interval_code AS exit_interval,
    xs.decision AS exit_decision,
    xs.original_decision AS exit_original_decision,
    xs.latest_price AS exit_signal_price,
    xs.generated_at AS exit_signal_generated_at,
    wmp.id AS wallet_managed_position_id,
    wmp.status AS wallet_managed_position_status,
    wmp.average_entry_price_usdt,
    wmp.stop_loss_usdt,
    wmp.take_profit_usdt,
    pea.id AS production_exit_audit_id,
    pea.close_trigger AS audit_close_trigger,
    pea.source_signal_id AS audit_source_signal_id,
    pea.source_signal_decision AS audit_source_signal_decision,
    pea.wallet_position_id AS audit_wallet_position_id,
    pea.close_explanation AS audit_close_explanation,
    pea.audited_at
FROM paper_position pp
LEFT JOIN trade_signal es ON es.id = pp.signal_id
LEFT JOIN trade_signal xs ON xs.id = pp.exit_signal_id
LEFT JOIN wallet_managed_position wmp ON wmp.entry_signal_id = pp.signal_id
LEFT JOIN production_exit_audit pea ON pea.paper_position_id = pp.id
WHERE pp.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(pp.id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137);

SELECT
    pp.id AS paper_position_id,
    wmp.id AS wallet_managed_position_id,
    wt.id AS wallet_trade_id,
    wt.signal_id,
    wt.execution_key,
    wt.symbol,
    wt.side,
    wt.quantity,
    wt.price_usdt,
    wt.decision_price_usdt,
    wt.execution_price_observed_at,
    wt.gross_amount_usdt,
    wt.fee_usdt,
    wt.net_amount_usdt,
    wt.cost_basis_usdt,
    wt.realized_pnl_usdt,
    wt.execution_reason,
    wt.status,
    wt.executed_at,
    wt.execution_message
FROM paper_position pp
LEFT JOIN wallet_managed_position wmp ON wmp.entry_signal_id = pp.signal_id
JOIN wallet_trade wt
  ON wt.signal_id IN (pp.signal_id, pp.exit_signal_id)
  OR (
      wmp.id IS NOT NULL
      AND wt.execution_key LIKE CONCAT('POSITION:', wmp.id, ':%')
  )
WHERE pp.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY pp.id, wt.executed_at, wt.id;

SELECT
    pp.id AS paper_position_id,
    pp.symbol,
    pp.closed_at,
    mpe.id AS market_price_event_id,
    mpe.observed_at,
    mpe.price,
    mpe.source,
    mpe.delivery_status,
    mpe.ordering_slot,
    mpe.outcome_reason,
    mpe.created_at
FROM paper_position pp
JOIN market_price_event mpe
  ON mpe.symbol = pp.symbol
 AND mpe.observed_at BETWEEN DATE_SUB(pp.closed_at, INTERVAL 15 SECOND)
                         AND DATE_ADD(pp.closed_at, INTERVAL 15 SECOND)
```

CURRENT TASK: ITERATION 5 - ENTRY QUALITY
Iteration 5 of 8. Self-review only.
Output FINDINGS, EVIDENCE, UNKNOWNS, and DB_REQUEST blocks as needed.
Label PROJECT EVIDENCE / EXTERNAL RESEARCH / AGENT INFERENCE.
## FINDINGS ƒ?" Iteration 5: Entry Quality

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Entry quality for frozen `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137** is presently **unverifiable**, not weak or defective. The permitted Codex DB-evidence folder contains no results, and no frozen position has been linked to its entry signal, executed BUY, or managed position.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Current code separates signal quality from entry quality. A bullish signal is not sufficient: at execution the system obtains a fresh price, recomputes entry quality against that price, and can cancel an otherwise approved BUY if price is already at/below the stop, at/above target, or classified as a chase.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Current entry-quality scoring uses only contemporaneous/persisted pre-entry evidence: rolling 30-minute signal prices; a fresh opportunity anchor/best price when applicable; execution-price expansion; ATR extension; risk/reward at execution price; opportunity age; recent rejection-zone evidence; high-volatility regime; and `STOP_EXPOSED` liquidity state. Score under 50 is `CHASE_ENTRY` and blocks an initial entry; higher scores cap initial allocation by tier.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Current execution validation additionally requires a 1m execution signal plus fresh 5m (20-minute) and 1h (3-hour) context, with profile-specific authority. This describes the checked-in source, not the historical release/configuration governing any frozen trade.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** `wallet_managed_position.entry_quality_score`, if present, cannot alone prove an original entryƒ?Ts quality: current code overwrites it on each `executeBuy`, including a progressive add. A valid reconstruction needs the first linked executed BUY, its observed execution price/time, and the signal/context evidence available before that point.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** The prior ICPUSDT log candidate remains unusable for this entry-quality study: it identifies managed-position ID `182`, not proven frozen `paper_position` ID `182`.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The supplied snapshot UTC and KSA values do not represent the same instant. The frozen IDs remain authoritative, but the snapshot clock must not be treated as a precise historical cutoff.

## EVIDENCE

- [AnalysisService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java:278) assembles and persists the contemporaneous analysis decision, context gates, component scores, price, ATR, stop, target, and risk/reward.

- [TradeExecutionValidationService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:62) requires the executable 1m signal and fresh 5m/1h context before profile approval.

- [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:865) defines the dynamic entry-quality calculation; [the initial guard](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1171) blocks scores below 50 and caps approved size.

- [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:377) revalidates against a fresh execution price before the wallet BUY and paper-position creation.

- [WalletAutoExecutionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletAutoExecutionService.java:139) writes the supplied score to the managed position; therefore it is mutable across later BUY stages.

- **[EXTERNAL RESEARCH]** None.

The durable link is via `paper_position.signal_id` ƒ+' `wallet_managed_position.entry_signal_id`, not equal numeric IDs. [ProductionExitAuditService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/audit/service/ProductionExitAuditService.java:37)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] Candidate only, not attributed:** logs previously identified an ICPUSDT stop-loss event:
  - Observation: `2026-10-11 22:49:33.460 UTC` / `2026-10-12 01:49:33.460 KSA`
  - Stop log: `2026-10-11 22:49:36.971 UTC` / `2026-10-12 01:49:36.971 KSA`
  - Committed outcome: `2026-10-11 22:49:36.974 UTC` / `2026-10-12 01:49:36.974 KSA`
  
  If linkage is later proven, current source describes this as an absolute-stop route, with mechanical wallet exit preceding paper-position closure. It is not evidence of a fault by itself. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:173)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] Protection root-cause testing must use accepted price evidence.** An `APPLIED` direct-price outcome commits with protection effects; stale, future, duplicate, or out-of-order observations are non-canonical. Candle OHLC cannot replace missing intrabar price-event evidence. [MarketPriceEventService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:58)

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] There are multiple possible close routes with different accounting guarantees.** Live TP/SL/profit-lock exits close wallet state before paper state; the signal-driven paper-close route persists the paper close first and separately attempts wallet exit. Therefore, a closed losing `paper_position` alone cannot establish executed net loss or a valid root cause. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:487)

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** No common failure mechanism can be responsibly inferred across the six losses. Possible classificationsƒ?"valid strategy loss, stop/profit-lock behavior, late/weak entry, execution/accounting discrepancy, or missing evidenceƒ?"remain competing hypotheses.

- **[EXTERNAL RESEARCH]** None. This iteration requires trade-specific durable evidence; general market research would not establish historical system behavior.

## EVIDENCE

## FINDINGS — Iteration 7 self-challenge

1. **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] No trade-specific conclusion is established.** The permitted `db/CODEX` evidence directory is empty. For frozen `paper_position` IDs `182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137`, root cause, entry quality, exit correctness, and P&L reconciliation remain unproven.

2. **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] The ICPUSDT stop-loss material is only an unattributed candidate, not evidence for frozen trade 182.** The logs identify `WalletManagedPosition` ID `182`, while the frozen entity is `paper_position` ID `182`. Current source links them through entry-signal identity, not matching numeric IDs. The stop log itself does not identify a paper-position ID.

3. **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] Prior timing wording needs correction.** For event `1837489`:
   - Observation: `2026-10-11 22:49:33.460 UTC` / `2026-10-12 01:49:33.460 KSA`
   - committed `APPLIED`: `2026-10-11 22:49:36.974 UTC` / `2026-10-12 01:49:36.974 KSA`
   - exact observed-to-commit interval: **3.514 seconds**, not 3.513.

   The logged protection transaction duration is `2438 ms`, and symbol-coordination wait is `0 ms`. This does **not** establish 3.514 seconds of processing delay: `observedAt` is an exchange observation time, while the remainder can include transport, callback scheduling, and uninstrumented work.

4. **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] `APPLIED` alone is insufficient to prove an executed close.** It proves the protection callback committed; source guidance says it may also represent a successful no-op. The nearby `Live STOP_LOSS executed` line makes an ICPUSDT stop plausible, but only a durable position/wallet/paper linkage can attribute it to a frozen trade.

5. **[AGENT INFERENCE] The earlier broad wallet-trade join is not conclusive enough for forensic attribution.** Joining `wallet_trade` through either signal IDs or an execution-key pattern can return multiple rows and must not alone establish a one-to-one lifecycle. First prove the paper-position → entry-signal → managed-position relationship, then reconcile each wallet row by its durable identifiers and chronology.

6. **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] Symbol-level log matching is particularly weak here.** The archived logs contain several ICPUSDT stop-loss/profit-lock/take-profit messages. A matching symbol, approximate time, or same numeric ID cannot replace the required durable linkage.

7. **[PROJECT EVIDENCE] The supplied snapshot clock remains internally inconsistent.** `2026-10-25 10:08:57 UTC` converts to `2026-10-25 13:08:57 KSA`, not the supplied `2026-10-23 11:36:38 KSA`. The six frozen IDs remain authoritative; no precise historical selection cutoff can be inferred.

## EVIDENCE

### PROJECT EVIDENCE

- `ProductionExitAuditService` finds a managed position by the paper position’s entry-signal ID; it does not use equal position IDs.
- `LivePositionProtectionService` records an `APPLIED` direct-price outcome after protection processing, while `PaperTradingService` has a distinct paper-first signal-close path.
- `WalletAutoExecutionService` overwrites `entryQualityScore` on each BUY, so a current managed-position score cannot alone establish original-entry quality.
- `TRADE_INVESTIGATION_AGENT.md` requires accepted price evidence, durable linkage, fee/quantity reconciliation, and UTC/KSA conversion for factual conclusions.
- Archived log sequence for ICPUSDT event `1837489` supports only the candidate timing stated above.

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I7-001  
Purpose: Verify the actual `paper_position` schema, preserve the frozen rows as the only study subjects, and test the stated selection rule without assuming a historical snapshot can be recreated. Returned timestamps are UTC.

SQL:
```sql
SHOW COLUMNS FROM crypto_ai.paper_position;

SELECT
    id,
    symbol,
    status,
    realized_pnl,
    opened_at,
    closed_at
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
WHERE id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137);

SELECT
    id,
    symbol,
    status,
    realized_pnl,
    closed_at
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
WHERE status = 'CLOSED'
  AND closed_at IS NOT NULL
  AND realized_pnl < 0
ORDER BY closed_at DESC
LIMIT 6;
```
